When the sum DOES drop below 100 (strict `<`, confirmed by a boundary scenario at exactly 100 NOT bypassing), the reference abandons priority AND health entirely, spreading flat by RAW HOST COUNT across every host in every tier — a live both-tiers-at-20%-healthy scenario gave a clean ~50/50-by-host-count split (32/32/32/32/32 and 28/28/28/28/28, INCLUDING unhealthy hosts), `lb_healthy_panic` incrementing on every request (the SAME existing counter — no new stat). envoy-go reproduces this bit-for-bit via a NEW `tierHealth` panic-disabled per-tier health view (AMEND-P1-COROLLARY — a constructor-time-only addition costing nothing against the zero-new-`Pick`-parameter design; a documented IMPROVEMENT over `locality.go`'s own accepted-but-unfixed "child-local-panic" coverage boundary from phase 52, now correctable because this SPEC's probe supplies the concrete evidence phase 52 lacked) plus a flat fallback child built with health=NIL (a deliberate departure from `localityWeightedLB`'s own flat child, since priorityLB's differently-shaped bypass condition cannot guarantee agreement with a leaf's own internal panic check the way phase 52's identical-computation shape could). **D-P4's cascade CONFIRMED per-tier, REFUTED + CORRECTED at the remainder-aggregation step (AMEND-P4):** a 3-tier decisive probe (P0=40%/P1=60%/P2=100% healthy, capacities 56/84/100) distinguished a "naive recursive-fraction" cascade (predicting P1=36.96%, P2=7.04%) from the CORRECT "cap each tier's load by its own capacity against the REMAINING budget" reading (predicting P1=44%, P2=0%) — observed P2=0.0% (0/300) decisively confirms the corrected reading; the two readings coincide at exactly 2 tiers (why the BRAINSTORM's hypothesis looked right there) but diverge at 3+. **D-P2 CONFIRMED both composition rejects as genuine departures, MORE SPECIFIC than anticipated (AMEND-P2):** the reference doesn't merely accept combining multi-tier `priority` with `locality_weighted_lb_config`/`lb_subset_config`, it MEANINGFULLY COMPOSES both (priority selects the tier first, locality-weighting or subset-selection divides load WITHIN it — live-confirmed via working 3-layer boot+traffic tests for both combinations). **D-P3 CONFIRMED** the proto surface (`LocalityLbEndpoints.priority` is a plain `uint32`, field 5, no wrapper-type ambiguity — simpler than phase 52's `overprovisioning_factor` wrapper-presence wrinkle, D-LW-OPF0, which `priorityLB` still REUSES verbatim for the `overprovisioning_factor` field itself) + a clean `go mod tidy` no-op (ZERO new dep). **D-P5 REFUTED DOWN to a confirmed ZERO stat delta** — the family's FOURTH zero-stat-delta LB phase (after phase-38.2/phase-35/phase-52) — no dedicated priority stat exists in the reference; only its own unrelated per-priority circuit-breaker buckets (`circuit_breakers.default`/`.high`). **D-P6 CONFIRMED** ~230-330 prod LoC / ~11-12 tasks, comfortably under the ADR-0045 gate — NO split (§3.0). An independent `general-purpose` spec-document-reviewer pass (mirroring the phase-52 precedent) found and the controller fixed three issues before landing: a wrong file:line citation (`extractEndpoints` is `manager.go:814-841`, not the initially-drafted `:771-795`); a dropped `hasOPF` absent-vs-explicit-zero distinction in the `newPriorityLB` sketch (now threads `(opf uint32, hasOPF bool)`, mirroring phase 52's D-LW-OPF0 exactly, reused verbatim rather than re-derived — an explicit `overprovisioning_factor: {value: 0}` is honored literally, not silently substituted with 140); and a `leafFactory` origin misattribution (it is defined in `subset.go:131`, merely reused by `locality.go`, not defined there — corrected in two places). **1 ADR anticipated:** ADR-0270 (a single-ADR reuse-shape phase, confirmed correct — §11.7 finds ZERO `Pick`-signature change, only constructor-time wiring; §Context drafted at this SPEC's §13, §Decision/§Consequences land at IMPL). A new `internal/cluster/locality.go` `localityWeightedLB` wrapper `loadBalancer` (the phase-38.1 `subsetLB` structural precedent, generalized from a route-resolved metadata match to a build-time-fixed locality identity) partitions a cluster's endpoints by `LocalityLbEndpoints.locality` identity and weighted-random-draws a locality per `Pick`, scaled by that locality's live healthy-host fraction via the confirmed `effective_weight = configured_weight × min(1, (overprovisioning_factor/100) × healthy_fraction)` formula (AMEND-LW3) — delegating to a per-locality child `loadBalancer` built by the EXISTING `buildLeafLB` factory + a `flat` all-endpoints fallback child for the panic-mode (AMEND-LW4) and zero-total-weight (AMEND-LW2) paths. UNLIKE `subsetLB`, needs **ZERO new `Pick` parameters** (D-LW7 CONFIRMED byte-for-byte). `Endpoint` grows `Locality LocalityID`/`LocalityWeight uint32` (the SECOND per-endpoint dimension after phase-38's `Metadata`), captured by an extended `extractEndpoints`; `manager.go`'s `buildCluster` gains a SECOND wrap-after-switch site (`sc` hoisted once above both), gated by TWO new envoy-go-strict rejects (`zone_aware_lb_config` unsupported; `lb_subset_config` combined with `locality_weighted_lb_config` rejected — both CONFIRMED live departures at SPEC). Two design upgrades surfaced during IMPL, both landed and documented: **D-LW-HEALTHALLOC** (allocating `clusterHealth` unconditionally for a locality-weighted cluster widens the CONDITION under which `registerClusterMetrics`'s pre-existing unconditional health-registration block fires — `membership_healthy`/`lb_healthy_panic` now also register for such a cluster with zero `health_checks`; a widened condition on two pre-existing stat NAMES, not a stat-surface COUNT change) and **D-LW-OPF0** (`newLocalityWeightedLB` threads `(opf uint32, hasOPF bool)` rather than a bare `uint32`, since the `*wrapperspb.UInt32Value` pointer is already in scope before `.GetValue()` — an explicit `overprovisioning_factor: 0` is honored LITERALLY, degrading the whole mechanism to flat fallback unconditionally, rather than silently defaulting to 140; an upgrade beyond the SPEC's own leaning-to-accept-the-departure default). A previously-undocumented structural finding (the child-local-panic coverage boundary — a locality's own leaf child evaluates its OWN `clusterHealth.inPanic` over its OWN endpoint sub-slice, independent of the wrapper's cluster-wide panic gate) is documented and unit-tested, not fixed (fixing it would require passing a per-locality "panic disabled" view into `buildLeafLB`'s children, contradicting the ZERO-new-`Pick`-parameter design). The new `test/fixtures/0095-lb-locality-weighted` cross-side differential (2 localities × 5 hosts, 2:1 weight) proves BOTH arms live: (a) an all-healthy static weight-ratio band and (b) a health-degradation shift band (reusing the phase-39 poll-to-convergence-and-warmup pattern), each 20/20 flake-free and `-race` clean — via TWO controller-reperformed deliberate breaks (skip-the-wrap; freeze-effective-weight-at-100%-healthy) against the frozen worktree HEAD, each failing exactly as predicted, then cleanly restored. Building this fixture surfaced and root-caused THREE genuine bugs (not papered over): an unset `no_traffic_healthy_interval` stalling arm(b)'s convergence poll (defaults to `no_traffic_interval`, 60s, not `interval`); a health-blind warmup gate (`toggleResponder` always answers 200 on the data path, so the original K-consecutive-200s check was a no-op — fixed by making it host-identity-aware); and a `-count=N` singleton-state leak across repeated fixture invocations in the same process (the memoized region-A `toggleResponder`s never reset to healthy — fixed by resetting inside `ensureRegionA()` on every call, before containers boot). A genuine PLAN gap (the `test/differential/runner_test.go` blank-import registration for fixture `0095`, never mentioned in any of the PLAN's 10 tasks — without it the fixture would have been silently skipped forever) was found and fixed by the controller before dispatching that task. Counts: stat surface **1200 UNCHANGED** (+0, CONFIRMED — the THIRD zero-stat-delta LB phase) / fixtures **96 → 97** (`0095-lb-locality-weighted`) / fuzzers **52 UNCHANGED** (+0 — no wire-decode surface) / BackendKind **38 UNCHANGED** (+0 — `0095` reuses the existing `HTTPEcho` family) / DECISIONS **ADR-0268 → ADR-0269** (next-free **ADR-0270**) / **ZERO new Go packages** (`locality.go` lives in the existing `internal/cluster`) / ZERO new go.mod modules (`go mod tidy -diff` EMPTY). The six gates GREEN: `go build ./...` OK; `gofmt -l .` empty; `golangci-lint run ./...` clean; `go vet ./...` clean; `go test ./... -count=1` all pass; the full **97-dir** differential `-count=1` AND `-race -count=1` both pass, with every observed failure isolate-re-run-confirmed as the known transient full-suite Docker-startup/port-contention flake pattern (`reference_differential_fullsuite_startup_flake`) — not a regression. **ROW 52 (`load-balancer-locality-weighted`) FLIPS `done`** — the SOLE leg (ADR-0106; NO parent rollup); the Load-balancing family STAYS OPEN (priority load balancing / panic thresholds remain deferred, not-yet-chartered candidates). **Doc:** `docs/envoy-go/DECISIONS.md` ADR-0269. The IMPL predecessor — `phase 52 PLAN done` (below).

when NO locality anywhere sets a weight, the mechanism degrades to flat round-robin (both-omitted → an even ~1:1 split). envoy-go unifies both outcomes with ONE rule: a locality's raw captured weight (0 if unset, zero substitution logic) naturally yields an empty weighted-draw bucket, and a zero TOTAL effective weight across every locality falls back to the same flat all-endpoints child panic mode uses. **AMEND-LW3 (CONFIRMATION of the hypothesized formula):** `effective_weight = configured_weight × min(1, (overprovisioning_factor/100) × healthy_fraction)` matched a live 6-point degradation curve (100→0% healthy) to within <1 percentage point at every measurement; an OPF-140-vs-100 A/B at an identical 60%-healthy state gave clearly different results (62.8% vs 54.0%), PROVING `ClusterLoadAssignment.Policy.overprovisioning_factor` (default 140) must be genuinely consumed, not hardcoded. **AMEND-LW4 (CONFIRMATION):** cluster-wide panic (the EXISTING, UNMODIFIED `clusterHealth.inPanic` gate) fully bypasses BOTH health-filtering and locality-weighting (both localities degraded to 1/5 healthy → traffic spread near-evenly ~49.4%/50.6% across all 10 hosts, not the configured 2:1). **AMEND-LW5 (REFUTATION DOWN):** the anticipated "small 0-2" stat-surface delta is a confirmed **ZERO** — a live `/stats` scrape found no dedicated locality-weighted stat group in the reference; `zone_aware_lb_config` alone is a fully legitimate, separately-working reference feature. Architecture (D-LW7 CONFIRMED): `internal/cluster/locality.go`'s `localityWeightedLB` — a wrapper (the phase-38 `subsetLB` structural precedent) needing **ZERO new `Pick` parameters**, forwarding `(hashKey, hasHash, match, hasMatch)` straight through to a per-locality child exactly as `subsetLB` already does; `Endpoint` grows `Locality`/`LocalityWeight` (the `Metadata` precedent); a second wrap-after-switch site in `manager.go`'s `buildCluster`. the span `name` = the request AUTHORITY [NOT the OTLP constant `"ingress"`], `timestamp`/`duration` = MICROseconds, 14 STRING `tags` [OTLP-16 MINUS `node_id`/`zone`], `localEndpoint{serviceName?,ipv4,port:0}`, an `"ss"` annotation, `id`==low64(`traceId`) for a fresh root; (D-TRACE-ZIPKIN-REJECT) a missing collector cluster **BOOT-REJECTS** [`envoy.tracers.zipkin: unknown cluster` → exit 1] — UNLIKE the permissive OTLP, so envoy-go's cluster-exists gate is REFERENCE-PARITY here [NOT a departure]; The BRAINSTORM predecessor — `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (squash `676e17cf`; the Upstream-robustness family CLOSED). ALSO superseded — the prior IMPL-done note:

- **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (2026-06-24) — the 10-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task), the six-gate GREEN, the full **82-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; **row 43 (`connection-pooling`) FLIPS `in-progress → done`** — ALL legs 43.1 + 43.2a + 43.2b landed ⇒ the **Upstream-robustness family (phases 39–43) CLOSES**). As built: **ADR-0254** — graceful GOAWAY-driven HTTP/2 upstream connection rotation (the H2-multiplex-pool drain lifecycle ADR-0253's minimal liveness DEFERRED). The codec exposes three read-only accessors over the existing `goawayCh`/`ctx` — `GoneAway() bool` + `GoneAwayCh() <-chan struct{}` + `Done() <-chan struct{}` (NO `cc.cancel()`, so an in-flight stream below `LastStreamID` still completes; the `Done()` accessor REQUIRED because `cc.ctx` is unexported from package `cluster`). A GOAWAY'd conn becomes DRAINING: (1) **admission-skip** — the two ESTABLISHED-conn sites (`findStreamHitLocked` + `h2PromoteLocked`'s stream-slot branch) add `&& !pc.cc.GoneAway()`; (2) **in-flight drain-close-on-last-stream** — `makeRelease`'s eager-close generalizes `pc.cc.Closed() && inFlight==0` → `(Closed() || GoneAway()) && inFlight==0`; (3) **idle prompt-close** — a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine (`watchDrain`, spawned in `dialAndPool` after the pool append) `select`s `GoneAwayCh()` vs `Done()` and evicts an IDLE draining conn promptly under `h2PoolMu` (re-checking `findPooledLocked != nil` — the double-evict guard); (4) **lazy replacement** via the established MISS path (`upstream_cx_http2_total` ++; permit re-homed). The rotation is observed ENTIRELY via `upstream_cx_http2_total` + the base `upstream_cx_*` (NO new GOAWAY-named stat — AMEND-H2B-1; the reference has no `goaway_received` + reads `http2.goaway_sent==0` on a graceful drain-close). The FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring: `cluster.<name>.http2.rx_reset` + `http2.tx_reset` (useH2-gated, **1185 → 1187**) injected via the variadic `h2.WithResetHooks(onRx, onTx func())` option applied PRE-`readLoop` (D-H2B-WIRING — a setter would race the running readLoop; the codec stays stats-agnostic, no `internal/stats` import) at `dialPooledH2To`; the kept `DialH2` passes nil. The codec increments at its existing RST sites (`dispatchFrame` `RSTStreamFrame` rx + `RoundTrip` `ctx.Done` CANCEL tx). NEW BackendKind **38** (`H2GoawayResponder` — a raw-`golang.org/x/net/http2`-framer h2c backend with `/__release` + `/__goaway`[NO_ERROR] + `/__rst`[INTERNAL_ERROR] gates; the kind-37 `H2HoldResponder` uses `h2c.NewHandler`/`http.Server` which CANNOT emit on-demand GOAWAY/RST frames). Differential **`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`** (cross-side EXACT — a six-stage sleepless drive: establish ⇒ in-flight drain ⇒ idle drain ⇒ rx_reset prong ⇒ tx_reset prong ⇒ quiesce; cross-side EXACT `upstream_cx_http2_total==2` + `http2.rx_reset==1` + `http2.tx_reset==1`). **Two documented FAITHFUL fixture-drive departures (recorded in ADR-0254 — NOT behavior divergences):** (A) `/__release` broadened to a backend-global broadcast; (B) `tx_reset` driven via a `per_try_timeout` (envoy-go dispatches H2 downstream streams on the connection-level ctx, so a downstream cancel doesn't propagate to the upstream `RoundTrip` ctx — the 42.2b "honors ctx.Deadline not ctx.Done on the upstream" precedent; the `per_try_timeout` reaches the SAME codec CANCEL site, cross-side EXACT). **The per-conn drain-watcher's idle-close is a recorded COVERAGE BOUNDARY:** 3 of 4 `-count=1` deliberate breaks bit (admission-skip → `cx_http2_total`, rx_reset, tx_reset); the watcher idle-close break does NOT bite because `makeRelease`'s eager-close masks it (the `/__goaway` control request rides the target conn as an in-flight stream, so its release eager-closes the conn before the watcher acts). The watcher remains CORRECT + ESSENTIAL in production for the truly-idle-GOAWAY case and is RIGOROUSLY UNIT-proven (a 1000-iteration contested race test + an idle-prompt-close test); a watcher-isolating differential is a recorded hardening item. 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. ADR-0254 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044. As-built counts: stat **1185 → 1187** (+2 — `http2.rx_reset` + `http2.tx_reset`, useH2-gated; non-H2 stays **1183**) / fixtures **81 → 82** (`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`) / fuzzers **42 documented → 43** (RECONCILED to the actual `^func Fuzz` count per `reference_fuzzer_count_docs_drift` — 43.2b adds none; D-H2B-FUZZER-RECONCILE) / BackendKind tail **37 → 38** (`H2GoawayResponder`) / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253 → ADR-0254** (next-free **ADR-0255**); ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. The full six-gate GREEN (build/vet/gofmt/lint clean; unit+integration GREEN; full 82-dir differential GREEN). **DEFERRED hardening items (recorded in ADR-0254):** `http2.goaway_sent` (would diverge — AMEND-H2B-3), `http2.stream_refused_errors` (reference-dormant), `min(local,peer)` peer-min enforcement, `nextStreamID` 2^31 retirement, + a watcher-isolating differential. **NEXT → a FRESH BRAINSTORM for a NEW subject** (the Upstream-robustness family is CLOSED — 0 candidates remain; the next phase opens a NEW family per the family-closure precedent; `superpowers:brainstorming`, a fresh worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`). corrected to the two ESTABLISHED-conn sites `findStreamHitLocked` + `h2PromoteLocked`'s stream-slot branch only] + a §3.5 "→82-dir" clarifier; the `cx_close_notify`/`cx_destroy_local` non-emission independently corroborated by grep). **The §11 D-H2B-* live-probe pins EXECUTED IN-SESSION 2026-06-24** against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network` (a shared bridge `h2bprobe`; an H1 downstream → h2c upstream cluster `c_h2` with `max_concurrent_streams: 100`; a raw-`golang.org/x/net/http2`-framer h2c backend with `/goaway` + `/rst_last` control gates; sequenced single-shot + `/stats`-delta scrapes; ran in a throwaway worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`, discarded after pinning). **THE HEADLINE EMPIRICAL REVERSAL (AMEND-H2B-1..4) — the probe REVERSED the brainstorm's stat scope DOWN:** (AMEND-H2B-1) a peer GOAWAY is observed ENTIRELY via the connection-lifecycle counters — `upstream_cx_close_notify` ++ IMMEDIATELY on receipt (drain mark), then for an IDLE conn a PROMPT local close (`upstream_cx_destroy`/`_destroy_local` ++, `cx_active` --) and for an IN-FLIGHT conn the conn HELD OPEN until the last in-flight stream completes then `upstream_cx_destroy_local` ++ (`_with_active_rq` STAYS 0 — graceful), replacement LAZY on the next request (`upstream_cx_http2_total` 1→2) — with **NO `http2.*` counter moving during a clean rotation** and **NO `goaway_received` counter existing** in the reference's 23-name `http2.*` subtree; (AMEND-H2B-2) of the brainstorm's FOUR candidate reset/goaway counters only **TWO** are LIVE + cross-side assertable — `http2.tx_reset` (downstream-cancel → the codec's existing `RoundTrip` ctx.Done `WriteRSTStream(id, CANCEL)`) + `http2.rx_reset` (backend RST → the codec's existing `dispatchFrame` `RSTStreamFrame` case → 503, conn survives) ⇒ register exactly those two, surface **1185 → 1187 (+2, REVISED DOWN from the brainstorm's ~1190/~5)**; (AMEND-H2B-3) `http2.goaway_sent` DEFERRED (the reference reads 0 through a full drain-close — envoy-go's `Close()`-emitted GOAWAY would DIVERGE non-zero) + `http2.stream_refused_errors` DEFERRED (reference-DORMANT: it proactively respects peer `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` + opens a conn-per-concurrent-cold-request prefetch, so it never RECEIVES a REFUSED_STREAM ⇒ a cross-side EXACT assert would be vacuous-at-0); (AMEND-H2B-4) the `0080` differential is cross-side EXACT under the sequenced-barrier drive — NO subject-EXACT + cross-ROBUST fallback needed (`cx_http2_total==2` post-rotation, `rx_reset==1`/`tx_reset==1` on their dedicated prongs). **The design (ADR-0254):** the codec exposes `GoneAway() bool` + `GoneAwayCh() <-chan struct{}` (read-only over the existing `goawayCh` — NO `cc.cancel()`, so an in-flight stream below `LastStreamID` still completes); the two ESTABLISHED-conn admission sites add `&& !pc.cc.GoneAway()`; `makeRelease`'s eager-close generalizes `pc.cc.Closed() && inFlight==0` → `(Closed() || GoneAway()) && inFlight==0` (the in-flight drain-close-on-last-stream); a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine spawned in `dialAndPool` after the `h2Pool[addr]` append (`select { <-cc.GoneAwayCh() [evict-if-idle under `h2PoolMu`, re-checking `findPooledLocked != nil`] ; <-cc.ctx.Done() [exit, evicted-by-another-path] }`) closes the IDLE draining conn promptly; lazy replacement via the established MISS path; the FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring injects the `http2.rx_reset`/`http2.tx_reset` handles at the `dialPooledH2To`→`NewClientConn` dial seam (codec increments at its existing RST sites). **PLAN D-questions:** D-H2B-{BACKEND [extend `H2HoldResponder` kind 37 with `/__goaway`+`/__rst` gates vs new kind 38], WATCHER-LIFECYCLE, WIRING [`NewClientConn` constructor-param vs setter; `DialH2` passes nil — nil-guarded increments], SPLIT-FINAL [43.2b anticipated flat ~8–10 tasks / ~150–220 LoC], FUZZER-RECONCILE [documented-42-vs-actual-43 — reconcile at this family-closing leg or carry forward], BYTESTAB [`0004`/`0079` stay GREEN — `0079` does not trigger GOAWAY], CXSTATS [which `upstream_cx_*` close/destroy counters envoy-go emits — grep shows it does NOT emit `cx_close_notify`/`cx_destroy_local`, so the `0080` EXACT prong asserts `cx_http2_total` + the two reset counters which BOTH sides emit]}. **ALL COUNTS UNCHANGED at this SPEC commit (docs-only):** stat surface **1185** / fixtures **81** / fuzzers **42** (documented; actual `^func Fuzz` 43) / BackendKind tail **37** / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253** (next-free **ADR-0254**). The SPEC predecessor — `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) BRAINSTORM done` (commit `b8ec7b4a`). ALSO superseded — the prior BRAINSTORM-done note:

- **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) BRAINSTORM done` (2026-06-24) — the SECOND/FINAL sub-leg of the H2-connection-pool leg brainstormed (lifecycle-state 0 → 1; row 43 STAYS `in-progress` — leg 43.1 done, 43.2a done, **43.2b brainstormed**). `docs/envoy-go/phases/43.2-h2-connection-pool/BRAINSTORM-43.2b.md` authored in the house BRAINSTORM shape + spec-document-reviewer APPROVED (first-iteration; ALL load-bearing code claims verified true against `h2pool.go`/`client.go` — the GOAWAY'd-but-alive conn is invisible to the 43.2a admission scan because `dispatchFrame`'s `GoAwayFrame` case closes `goawayCh` + finishes streams above `LastStreamID` but does NOT `cc.cancel()`, so `Closed()` stays false; 2 advisory recs, 1 FOLDED into D-H2B-WATCHER-LIFECYCLE [`cc.ctx.Done()` is the natural "evicted-by-another-path" watcher signal + re-check `findPooledLocked != nil` before evicting to dodge the double-evict race with `EvictH2ConnOnError`/`makeRelease`]). **The design forks settled via live dialogue:** (Q-scope → CORE-ONLY) GOAWAY rotation + the behavior-driven `http2.*` reset/goaway stats ONLY; `min(local,peer)` enforcement (AMEND-H2-5) + `nextStreamID` 2^31-exhaustion retirement (M-12/Task-11) DEFERRED as recorded hardening items (keeps 43.2b a single flat leg under the ADR-0045 gate — no further sub-split). (Q-drain-detection → WATCHER) a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine spawned at pool insertion in `dialAndPool` (`select { <-cc.GoneAwayCh() ; <-cc.ctx.Done() }`) that on GOAWAY marks the conn draining + evicts-if-idle — chosen over poll-on-admission (an IDLE GOAWAY'd conn has no `releaseStream` to ever fire + ctx never cancels ⇒ a slow conn leak) + over a codec `onGoAway` callback (inverts pool→codec layering). (Q-stats-scope → BEHAVIOR-SUBSET) register only the `http2.*` counters the codec drives live: candidates `http2.goaway_sent`/`rx_reset`/`tx_reset`/`stream_refused_errors` (every one LIVE + assertable) — chosen over the full ~24-name reference subtree (≈20 would register dead-at-0 — the codec lacks the flood/messaging-error paths) + over goaway_sent-only. (Q-stat-wiring → INJECT-HANDLES) the pool injects the cluster's `http2.*` stat handles into the `ClientConn` at dial (the FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring — 43.2a drove ALL stats from the pool layer) — chosen over codec-side atomic accumulators the pool reads-and-deltas (lossy for live reset counters). (Q-differential-strategy → SEQUENCED-EXACT-FIRST) drive the rotation as discrete poll-to-converge phases behind release barriers targeting cross-side EXACT (fire streams → barrier → trigger GOAWAY → poll the drained conn out of admission + the `http2.*` deltas → fire a fresh request → poll the replacement conn in → release → drain), with a subject-EXACT + cross-ROBUST fallback per-count if the SPEC probe shows a residual GOAWAY-vs-next-request race — chosen over subject-EXACT-upfront + over behavioral-only. **The `0080-h2-goaway-rotation` differential** MUST use an H2 (TLS+ALPN-h2) DOWNSTREAM listener (the 0004 PKI shape — the H2 pool engages ONLY on an H2 downstream, `reference_h2_pool_downstream_codec_gate`); the GOAWAY-capable backend (extend `H2HoldResponder` BackendKind 37 with a `/__goaway` gate vs a new BackendKind 38) is a PLAN D-question (D-H2B-BACKEND — the D-H2-BACKEND precedent). **SPEC live-probe pins (D-H2B-{STATS,ROTATION,EXACT,GOAWAYSENT})** to execute against `contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network` (the exact `http2.*` names + the `+N`; the reference's lazy-replacement `upstream_cx_*`/`cx_destroy` sequence; per-count cross-side determinism; whether `goaway_sent` is driven by OUR `Close()`-emitted GOAWAY [a peer-GOAWAY'd conn has `goawaySent==false` ⇒ Close still emits]). **PLAN D-questions:** D-H2B-{BACKEND, WATCHER-LIFECYCLE, WIRING [constructor-param vs setter; does `DialH2` pass nil], SPLIT-FINAL, FUZZER-RECONCILE [the documented-42-vs-actual-43 `^func Fuzz` drift — reconcile at this family-closing leg or carry forward], BYTESTAB [`0004`/`0079` stay GREEN — `0079` does not trigger GOAWAY]}. **ALL COUNTS UNCHANGED at this BRAINSTORM commit (docs-only):** stat surface **1185** / fixtures **81** / fuzzers **42** (documented; actual `^func Fuzz` 43) / BackendKind tail **37** / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253** (next-free **ADR-0254**). Anticipated at the 43.2b IMPL: stat **~1190** (+~5, the behavior-driven `http2.*` subset — EXACT `+N` SPEC-pinned) / fixtures **82** (`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`) / fuzzers **42** (or 43 if reconciled) / BackendKind **37** (extend) or **38** (new) / DECISIONS **ADR-0254** (ANCHORS the GOAWAY-rotation leg); ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. **Row 43 flips `done` at the 43.2b IMPL** (ALL legs 43.1 + 43.2a + 43.2b landed — ADR-0106 + `reference_roadmap_split_phase_row_done`) ⇒ the **Upstream-robustness family (phases 39–43) CLOSES** (0 candidates remain — the next phase opens a NEW family). **NEXT → the 43.2b SPEC** (`superpowers:brainstorming` predecessor → the §-level SPEC live-probe block D-H2B-*; anchor the ADR-0254 §Context; a docs-only commit; a fresh worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees` if any code-adjacent probe artifacts land — the doc itself commits DIRECT on master). The BRAINSTORM predecessor — `phase 43.2a (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (commit `30b3aad1`). ALSO superseded — the prior IMPL-done note:

- **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2a (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (2026-06-23) — the 12-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task + two-stage review), the six-gate GREEN, the full **81-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; row 43 STAYS `in-progress` — leg 43.1 done, **43.2a done**, 43.2b pending). As built: **ADR-0253** — a per-endpoint HTTP/2 multiplex `ClientConn` pool over the 43.1 permit substrate (ADR-0252), **SUPERSEDING ADR-0056** (per-request-fresh H2 dial). `Cluster.h2Pool map[string][]*pooledH2Conn` (keyed by `ep.Addr()`, guarded by `h2PoolMu`) holds multiplexed `*h2.ClientConn`s; a new conn opens only when every existing conn is saturated at the cluster's OWN `http2_protocol_options.max_concurrent_streams` (the LOCAL cap C — AMEND-H2-1, the headline live finding: the reference keys multi-conn growth off the local cap, NOT peer SETTINGS ⇒ `ceil(K/C)` deterministic, cross-side EXACT-able), default `1<<30` (AMEND-H2-3, single-conn multiplex). A SEPARATE stream-aware pending queue (woken on stream-free via `h2PromoteLocked` OR conn-close; bounded by `max_pending_requests`; queue-full → `errConnPoolOverflow` → 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow`); `tryAcquireConnSlot` (NON-blocking) keeps H2 OUT of the 43.1 `connPool.waiters`. **Two key as-built mechanisms the SPEC under-specified (mid-IMPL fixes):** (Task 9.5) **connect-time dial coalescing** — a "connecting" tier (`Cluster.h2Connecting`, `connectingH2Conn{ready, cc, err, promised}`) so K CONCURRENT acquires ATTACH to establishing conns rather than each dialing ⇒ exactly `ceil(K/C)` conns under burst (matching the reference; without it the pool over-dialed to K); (Task 7.5) **a single ctx-aware LB pick** threaded through the dial seam (ONE `c.lb.Pick(hashKeyFrom(ctx),…,subsetMatchFrom(ctx),…)` firing exactly once per acquire, fixing an earlier ctx-blind double-pick defect) ⇒ conns keyed+attributed to the dialed endpoint + hash/subset/source_ip affinity honored. The router rewire moved BOTH `router_h2.go` H2 sites off `DialH2`+`defer cc.Close()` onto `AcquireH2Stream`+`release()`+evict-on-error; the legacy `doH2` was proven DEAD + REMOVED (completing the ADR-0056 supersession IN CODE). Minimal liveness only (`Closed()`-skip + evict-on-RoundTrip-error); GOAWAY rotation + the `http2.*` reset/goaway stats + `min(local,peer)` peer-min are DEFERRED to 43.2b (ADR-0254). **A SPEC §8.1 topology correction:** envoy-go selects the router action by the DOWNSTREAM listener codec — the H2 pool engages ONLY on an H2 (TLS+ALPN-h2) downstream listener (there is NO H1-down→H2-up bridge); `0079` uses an H2 downstream accordingly. **2 new stats** (useH2-gated): `upstream_cx_http2_total` (counter) + `http2.streams_active` (gauge); NO `upstream_cx_http2_active` (AMEND-H2-2) ⇒ an H2 cluster emits **1185**, a non-H2 cluster stays at **1183** (confirmed by `TestRegisterClusterMetrics_H2Stats`). Differential **`0079-h2-multiplex-pool`** (cross-side EXACT: `ceil(K/C)` conns + `http2.streams_active==K` both sides; an overflow prong → downstream 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` delta) + NEW BackendKind **37** (`H2HoldResponder` — h2c hold-and-release, `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS: 1000`); 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (all bit) + 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. ADR-0253 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044; ADR-0056 status header edited to `SUPERSEDED by ADR-0253`. As-built counts: stat **1183 → 1185** (+2) / fixtures **80 → 81** (`0079`) / fuzzers **42** (documented; actual `^func Fuzz` 43 per `reference_fuzzer_count_docs_drift` — 43.2a adds none) / BackendKind tail **36 → 37** (`H2HoldResponder`) / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0252 → ADR-0253** (next-free **ADR-0254**); ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. The full six-gate GREEN (build/vet/gofmt/lint clean; unit+integration GREEN; full 81-dir differential GREEN — 4 transient `subject ready: EOF` startup flakes [`reference_differential_fullsuite_startup_flake`] confirmed transient by isolate-re-run + full re-run, NOT regressions; the only pre-existing H2 fixture `0004` probes `/ready` not `/stats` so conn-reuse is unobserved by it). **Row 43 STAYS `in-progress`** (flips `done` only when ALL legs 43.1 + 43.2a + 43.2b land — ADR-0106 + `reference_roadmap_split_phase_row_done`). **NEXT → the 43.2b leg BRAINSTORM** (`superpowers:brainstorming` — graceful GOAWAY-driven conn rotation + drain-close-on-last-stream + the `http2.*` reset/goaway stats + a GOAWAY-emitting H2 differential backend + the `min(local,peer)` peer-min enforcement; ADR-0254; a fresh worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`); at the 43.2b IMPL row 43 flips `done` + the Upstream-robustness family CLOSES. A connection-creation permit is try-acquired at the two creation boundaries (`Cluster.Dial` raw-dial + the `AcquireH1` pool-MISS); a pool HIT reuses an already-counted conn (no permit). At the cap the request PENDS (bounded by `max_pending_requests`), woken via DIRECT permit-handoff on `connWithGauge.Close` (`connDec` → `pool.releaseConn` → pop head + send) or on `PutIdleH1` idle-return (CLOSE-and-wake when a waiter is queued, routing BOTH wake sources through the single permit mechanism); ctx-cancel re-locks + `removeWaiterLocked`, and if already dequeued drains + gives the permit back (raced-permit-never-leaks). Queue-full → `cluster.IsConnPoolOverflow` sentinel → fail-fast 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` (the shared phase-41 counter). **The load-bearing DEPARTURE (AMEND-CP1 — `reference_max_connections_soft_breaker`):** the reference's `max_connections` is a SOFT breaker (`cx_active` can exceed the cap with timing slop); envoy-go implements a CLEAN HARD-CAP as a documented departure recorded in ADR-0252. Each in-flight attempt is a goroutine (body replay + `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` + `matches` classify + the CB-admission/`RecordUpstreamResult`/`retry_budget` seams) with a per-attempt HEDGE-TRIGGER timer (`time.AfterFunc` at `per_try_timeout`) that on expiry launches a budget-counted hedge LEAVING the original in flight; a BUFFERED first-acceptable-wins collector returns the first non-retriable result + cancels/drains losers (no leak, `-race`-clean). The `hedge_policy` parsed in `parseHedgePolicy` (`hcm/config.go`) with the vhost fallback + carried on `clusterRouteAction.hedgePolicy` (`hcm/actions.go`); the `hp *HedgePolicy` threaded through the `H{1,2}ClusterAction` signatures. **The load-bearing invariants held as-built:** (AMEND-H1 — #1) a hedged per-try-timeout increments `upstream_rq_retry` (+ `_retry_backoff_exponential`, NO `time.Sleep`), NEVER `upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` (which STAYS **0** under hedge mode — the per-try-timeout is a hedge-LAUNCH not an abandonment; the hedge path uses a HEDGE-TRIGGER timer NOT the 42.2a cancelling-deadline-+-discriminator); (AMEND-H2) `upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded` under hedging = "no more hedges can launch" NOT a final failure — the in-flight original can still WIN with a 200 (hedging RECOVERS a slow request where 42.2a sequential ABANDONS it to a 504); (D-S422B-2 — a recorded departure) the H1/H2 loser-cancel ASYMMETRY [`doH1ClusterAction` honors only `ctx.Deadline()` ⇒ an H1 loser completes on backend-response (the `0077` `/__release` guarantees termination, NO active H1-loser socket reset); `doH2ClusterAction`'s `RoundTrip(ctx)` honors `ctx.Done()` ⇒ an H2 loser aborts promptly; the driver code UNCHANGED, reused concurrently]; (PLAN I1) the `time.AfterFunc` callback is a PURE non-blocking `hedgeReq <- struct{}{}` send, the COLLECTOR LOOP the SOLE mutator of `hedgesLaunched`/`limitExceededOnce` + SOLE caller of `Inc*`/`TryAcquireRetry`/`launch` (channels BUFFERED to `int(hp.InitialRequests)+maxHedges+1` ⇒ no loser blocks on send ⇒ no leak; `-race`-clean); (D-S422B-3/AMEND-H4) the `initial_requests`/`additional_request_chance` fan-out is reference-no-op'd ⇒ SUBJECT-SIDE (UNIT-tested — NO `0078` dir), each fan-out attempt `TryAcquireRetry`-gated BEFORE the goroutine spawn (acquire-gates-spawn caps the live goroutine count under the `retry_budget`); - **prior active-phase:** `phase 42.2a (per_try_timeout) IMPL done` (2026-06-21) — the 11-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task + two-stage review), the six-gate GREEN, the full **78-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; row 42 STAYS `in-progress`). As built: **ADR-0250** — `RetryPolicy.per_try_timeout` (field 3), a per-attempt deadline over the 42.1 retry loop; the project's FIRST request-scoped deadline (envoy-go does NOT implement the global `RouteAction.timeout`). The 42.1 `retryExecutorH1`/`retryExecutorH2` (`internal/filter/http/router/retry.go`) wrap each `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` call in a child `context.WithTimeout(ctx, rp.perTryTimeout)` (when `>0`); on the child deadline expiring while the PARENT ctx is alive, the attempt is a per-try-timeout — a synthesized **504** (overriding the H1 driver's 502 / the H2 `Status:0` CANCEL sentinel), `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout()`, retried via `perTryTimeoutRetriable()` (`matches(504,false) || retryReset`) under {5xx, gateway-error, retriable-status-codes∋504, **reset**} — NOT `connect-failure`-alone, so `parseRetryOn` SPLITS the 42.1-fused `connect-failure`/`reset` into `retryConnectFail` + a new `retryReset` (the 5th `retryOnBits` value; `matches` localOrigin arm `(retryConnectFail|retryReset)` preserves 42.1 dial-refusal-under-both). **The discriminator is RACE-ROBUST** (an IMPL-discovered refinement over the SPEC's simpler `errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), DeadlineExceeded)`, which was reproducibly racy under `-race`: the H1 driver propagates the child deadline onto the upstream socket, so the socket-I/O-timeout-broken read can return BEFORE context's timer stamps `Err()`): `timedOut := perTryTimeout>0 && ctx.Err()==nil && <failure-guard> && (errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), DeadlineExceeded) || !time.Now().Before(attemptDeadline))` where `<failure-guard>` is `resp.localOrigin` (H1) / `resp.localOrigin || resp.Status==0` (H2 — the timeout manifests as the `Status:0` sentinel, localOrigin=false) so a clean upstream reply is NEVER misclassified, the wall-clock fallback is monotonic-clock-immune to the timer lag, and it is read BEFORE the explicit `cancel()` (NOT defer — no timer-leak); the PARENT-alive guard preserves the 42.1 H2 `Status:0` client-cancel-not-retried invariant. The `per_try_timeout` is parsed in `parseRetryPolicy` (`hcm/config.go`, guarded `.AsDuration()`) onto a WIDENED `NewRetryPolicy` (6th param); `≤0`/unset ⇒ no bound (byte-stable), a NEGATIVE value ⇒ a route-scoped reject (`route: %q: retry_policy: per_try_timeout must not be negative`, the shared `ErrMsgPerTryTimeoutNegative` const). The **`upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` counter** is the 6th `retryStats` member via `EnsureRetryStats` (`cluster.go`) — registered on EVERY retry cluster (emit-0 where no `per_try_timeout`), ++ per timed-out attempt. The driver code is UNCHANGED (the H1 driver already `SetDeadline`s the socket `router.go:659-661`; the H2 driver already returns `Status:0` on a ctx deadline `router_h2.go:111`); NO `circuitbreaker.go` change. **Byte-identical when `per_try_timeout ≤ 0`** (the 77→78 differential is the regression anchor). DEPARTURE (D-S422A-8): the H1-records-a-local-origin-502-on-timeout vs H2-records-none asymmetry (the `0076` asserts neither `upstream_rq_502`/`upstream_rq_5xx`). Differential **`0076-per-try-timeout`** (cross-side: a SINGLE held `BlockingHoldResponder` host past a small `per_try_timeout` ⇒ deterministic exhaustion to a final 504; `upstream_rq_per_try_timeout==N+1` + `upstream_rq_retry==N` + `_retry_limit_exceeded==1` + `upstream_rq_total==N+1`, cross-side-EXACT [single host, offset-irrelevant]; `/__release` drains the parked attempts; NO new BackendKind); 2 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (A: per_try_timeout not threaded ⇒ the held read hangs to a transport timeout; B: `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout` no-op ⇒ the cross-side delta assert fails) + 20/20 flake-free. ADR-0250 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044 (refined to reflect the as-built race-robust discriminator). As-built counts: stat surface **1173 → 1181** (+8 = +2 on `0075`'s two retry clusters + +6 for `0076`'s new `c_ptt` cluster) / fixtures **77 → 78** (`0076`) / fuzzers **42** (UNCHANGED) / BackendKind tail **36** (UNCHANGED — REUSE `BlockingHoldResponder`) / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0249 → ADR-0250** (next-free **ADR-0251**). ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. **Row 42 STAYS `in-progress`** (the three-leg row flips `done` only when ALL THREE legs 42.1+42.2a+42.2b land — ADR-0106 + `reference_roadmap_split_phase_row_done`); the 42.2a IMPL records the leg LANDED, 42.2b hedging is the remaining leg. The Upstream-robustness family STAYS OPEN. **Next → the 42.2b (hedging) BRAINSTORM** (`superpowers:brainstorming` — the concurrent `hedgeExecutor` + `hedge_on_per_try_timeout` + the `initial_requests`/`additional_request_chance` fan-out over the 42.2a per-attempt deadline; ADR-0251). The §11 **D-H-{PROTO,TIMEOUT,RETRY,STATS,DIFFERENTIAL}** per-try-timeout pins EXECUTED IN-SESSION live against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network` (a shared bridge `ptprobe` + a STRICT_DNS `mccutchen/go-httpbin` `/delay/N` backend + a dead `:9999` host + a gateway Envoy with per-try-timeout routes varying `retry_on`; single-shot `/stats`-delta probes + `--mode validate` rejects). **SPEC-firmed (AMEND-PT1..7):** (PT1) a per-try-timeout ⇒ a synthesized **504**, retried iff `retry_on` ∈ {5xx, gateway-error, retriable-status-codes∋504, **reset**} — NOT `connect-failure`-alone, NOT empty (single-slow-host probe: 5xx/gateway-error/reset → 3 attempts, connect-failure/none → 1); a genuine dial-refusal retries under BOTH reset AND connect-failure (dead-backend probe delta=2 each) ⇒ 42.2a SPLITS `reset` into its own `retryReset` bit (the `localOrigin` dial-failure path retries under `retryConnectFail|retryReset`, PRESERVING 42.1); the per-try-timeout predicate = `matches(504,false) || retryReset`. (PT2) the counter `cluster.<n>.upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` (++ per timed-out ATTEMPT) folds into `EnsureRetryStats` (the 6th `retryStats` member) ⇒ +1 per retry cluster; surface 1173 → **~1181** (NOT the BRAINSTORM's +1: 0075's TWO retry clusters +1 each = +2, 0076's new six-counter cluster = +6). (PT3) envoy-go does NOT implement the global `RouteAction.timeout` (code-confirmed) ⇒ `per_try_timeout` is the project's FIRST request-scoped deadline; NO `min(per_try, global)` clamp; the global route timeout stays DEFERRED; `per_try_timeout ≤ 0s`/unset ⇒ no bound (byte-stable). (PT4) the per-try-timeout-vs-client-cancel discriminator: the executor wraps each attempt with `attemptCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(parentCtx, perTryTimeout)`, and AFTER the driver returns checks `parentCtx.Err()==nil && errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), context.DeadlineExceeded)` ⇒ a per-try-timeout (override the H1 502 / H2 `Status:0` to a synthesized 504, count, classify); ELSE the 42.1 behavior holds (the H2 `Status:0` PARENT client-cancel stays un-retried — the `TestRetryExecutorH2_CtxCancelNotRetried` invariant). The H1 driver ALREADY propagates the ctx deadline to the socket (`router.go:659-661`); the H2 driver ALREADY treats any ctx deadline as the `Status:0` CANCEL sentinel (`router_h2.go:111`) — the driver code is UNCHANGED. (PT5) a NEGATIVE `per_try_timeout` is rejected by the reference's generic protobuf duration-positivity check (`Invalid duration: Expected positive duration`); `0s` is valid (no bound); envoy-go mirrors with a thin reject OR clamps-to-no-bound (a PLAN call); NO new fuzzer. (PT6) differential `0076-per-try-timeout`: a SINGLE held `BlockingHoldResponder` host (held > a small `per_try_timeout` T) ⇒ DETERMINISTIC exhaustion to a final **504** with cross-side-EXACT counts (`upstream_rq_per_try_timeout==N+1`, `upstream_rq_retry==N`, `upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded==1`, `upstream_rq_total==N+1`); offset-irrelevant (single host); the T delay is feature-inherent NOT a flake-sleep; `/__release` drains at the end; NO new BackendKind. The admission seam: a synchronous `TryAcquireRequest()` at `doH1ClusterAction` (`router.go:591`) + `doH2ClusterAction` (`router_h2.go:60`) — after `IncUpstreamRqTotal`, before the upstream call — with the overflow early-return (`ActionResponse{Status:503,...}`) PRECEDING the `defer ReleaseRequest()` (an over-budget request never releases a slot it didn't acquire → no double-release); release fires on every other exit path. On exhaustion: fail-fast `503` + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow`++ + `rq_open`=1; NO `IncStatusClass` on the overflow path (the dedicated overflow counter is the signal; avoids a speculative `rq_5xx` cross-side mismatch). (CB2) a `max_requests` overflow increments **`upstream_rq_pending_overflow`** — the SAME counter as `max_pending_requests`; there is NO `upstream_rq_overflow` (the two request budgets differ only by gauge `rq_open` vs `rq_pending_open`). (CB3) stat surface **+14 → 1163** — the full parity block: 10 per-priority `*_open` gauges (`cx_open`/`cx_pool_open`/`rq_open`/`rq_pending_open`/`rq_retry_open` × {default, high}) + 4 cluster overflow counters (`upstream_cx_overflow`/`upstream_cx_pool_overflow`/`upstream_rq_pending_overflow`/`upstream_rq_retry_overflow`), scoped to `circuit_breakers` clusters (existing fixtures unaffected; a recorded departure — the reference emits the overflow counters on ALL clusters); only `default.rq_open` + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` go LIVE, the other 12 emit 0 (the AMEND-OD3-4 emit-0-for-parity precedent); `remaining_*` gauges DEFERRED (`track_remaining`-gated). (CB4) overflow ⇒ `503` + `UO` flag + `upstream_reset_before_response_started{overflow}`; envoy-go emits the 503 via the existing local-reply path + sets `UO` where the `%RESPONSE_FLAGS%` accesslog surface allows (a PLAN/IMPL pin); the differential asserts the robust 503-status + stats pair, NOT the access-log line. (CB5) effective defaults 1024/1024/1024/3/∞ (via the `remaining_*` gauges) — `max_requests` defaults 1024 ⇒ the breaker is OFF unless explicitly small. (CB6) the PGV reject surface is THIN — only `Thresholds.priority` enum-range + `RetryBudget.budget_percent ∈ [0,100]` bite (budget `UInt32Value`s unbounded; duplicate-priority + `per_host_thresholds` accepted); envoy-go mirrors the 2 PGV rejects + adds a strict duplicate-priority reject + silent-ignores `per_host_thresholds`; NO new fuzzer (config-parse, not wire-decode). As built: **ADR-0247** — the STATISTICAL passive-outlier detectors over the 40.1/40.2 substrate (ADR-0245/0246, REUSED UNCHANGED): the **success_rate detector** (`evalSuccessRate` — the eligible-host set [≥ `success_rate_minimum_hosts`, each ≥ `success_rate_request_volume`] + the cross-host mean + the **POPULATION stddev** + the `mean − (success_rate_stdev_factor/1000)×stddev` ejection threshold; a negative threshold is a no-op; ejects-by-default — `enforcing_success_rate` default 100) + the **failure_percentage detector** (`evalFailurePercentage` — the integer cross-multiplied `(total−success)*100 >= threshold*total` `>=` boundary over the eligible set; detect-only-by-default — `enforcing_failure_percentage` default 0). (HC4) the full reference stat set pinned [8 `health_check.*` + `membership_{change,healthy,degraded,excluded,total}` + `lb_healthy_panic` + `update_no_rebuild`]; **envoy-go MVP +7 → stat surface 1132** [`health_check.{attempt,success,failure,network_failure}` counters + `health_check.healthy` gauge + `membership_healthy` gauge + `lb_healthy_panic` counter; emitted only on clusters with `health_checks` → existing fixtures unaffected]; the deferred-feature stats {passive_failure/verify_cluster/degraded/membership_change/degraded/excluded/update_no_rebuild} are recorded departures. (HC5) `healthy_panic_threshold` default 50%, **strict `<`** (exactly 50% does NOT panic — verified `lb_healthy_panic: 0` at 1-of-2 healthy; a single-dead-host cluster 503'd + `lb_healthy_panic` 0→1); panic routes to ALL hosts → `0066` uses 2-live/1-dead (66%) to assert FILTERING not panic. (HC6) the unhealthy host is filtered from rotation (6/6 requests served by the live host, no 503); behavior-neutral when all healthy. (HC7) `0066-health-check-http` = 2 live + 1 dead, poll `/stats` to convergence then 100%-live assertion; single 39.1 leg (~14–20 tasks). (HC8) `go mod tidy -diff` EMPTY → ZERO new module; oneof fields http=8/tcp=9/grpc=11; `google.golang.org/grpc v1.70.0` ALREADY a direct dep → 39.2 gRPC uses `grpc_health_v1` directly. The **ADR-0242 + ADR-0243 §Context drafts ANCHORED** in SPEC §13 (DECISIONS tail STAYS ADR-0241; bodies at the 39.1 IMPL per ADR-0044). spec-document-reviewer APPROVED. - **prior active-phase:** `phase 39 (upstream-health-check) BRAINSTORM done` (2026-06-14) — the FIRST Upstream-robustness-family row and the first NEW feature family opened since §9 (Network filters) closed at phase 33 (phases 34–38 were all Load-balancing-family rows). Charters `Cluster.health_checks` (`[]core.config.core.v3.HealthCheck`, proto field 8) — **active** upstream health checking (periodic outbound probes that mark hosts healthy/unhealthy) over an HTTP + TCP + gRPC checker roster — plus the project's FIRST per-host **health-state dimension** and the health-aware LB pick that makes all six LB constructs route only to healthy hosts (with a panic-threshold fallback). The family **keystone**: the host health-state primitive (i) unblocks the open Load-balancing family's 3 health-gated candidates {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds}, and (ii) is the substrate the sibling rows {outlier detection, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, connection pooling} compose with. **Q-decisions:** (Q0) NEW family = Upstream robustness (chosen over xDS/gRPC/HTTP3 — the foundational reliability layer that unblocks the health-gated LB candidates); (Q0b) first sub-project = active health checks (the keystone — establishes the health boundary; chosen over the cleaner-to-differential retries/circuit-breakers); (Q1) checker roster = HTTP + TCP + gRPC, split by codec — **39.1** = the keystone (host health-state dimension + the cluster-level checker runtime [the project's FIRST background runtime — `Manager.StartHealthChecks(ctx)` boot start + `Manager.Drain()` cancel/wait] + the HTTP probe codec + the health-aware LB across all six constructs) + the `0066-health-check-http` differential; **39.2** = the TCP + gRPC checkers reusing the 39.1 substrate; (Q2) health-aware LB = **Approach A: pick-time filter + panic fallback** (LB built once over all hosts; `Pick` skips unhealthy + re-picks; ring_hash/maglev walk-to-next-healthy preserving key stability; `healthy_panic_threshold` default 50% fallback-to-all), with the health view **build-time-injected** (host health is CLUSTER state, NOT a per-request `Pick` widening — the deliberate CONTRAST with the ADR-0235 hash-key / ADR-0239 subset-match ctx-carry pick-inputs; the `Pick` signature + exported `Cluster` surface stay BYTE-STABLE; behavior-neutral when all healthy → every existing fixture stays byte-identical). A NEW `health_checks` parse-reject surface (envoy-go's own byte-stable rejects; D-HC2). Differential `0066-health-check-http` (a cluster with a LIVE + a DEAD unbound host; POLL `/stats` until the dead host is unhealthy on BOTH sides [no fixed sleep], THEN assert 100% of traffic on the live host + cross-side `health_check.*`/`membership_*` parity; 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks; ≥20-run flake-free). **2 ADRs anticipated:** ADR-0242 (the host health-state dimension + the active-HC runtime) + ADR-0243 (the health-aware LB pick — Approach A); the phase-36/38 two-ADR shape; §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. - **prior active-phase:** `phase 38.2 (weighted-clusters) IMPL done` (2026-06-14) — the 10-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven, the six-gate GREEN. As built: **ADR-0241** — the **weighted-cluster routing primitive** (`RouteAction.weighted_clusters`) + the `ClusterWeight.metadata_match` merge composition. The project's **FIRST per-request cluster-SELECTION primitive** (the SECOND `RouteAction.ClusterSpecifier` arm; every prior route resolves a SINGLE cluster at config-build). A route fans across N weighted clusters, ONE chosen per request by integer weight — weighted-RANDOM via a per-action `weightedSelector` (cumulative weights + a `newPCGRNG()` draw in `[0, Σweight)` + a cumulative walk — the FIRST use of the `randomLB` per-request RNG model OUTSIDE the cluster LB). `total_weight` is DEPRECATED upstream and IGNORED (the client uses the SUM). Each `ClusterWeight.metadata_match` MERGES over the route-level `RouteAction.metadata_match` (entry precedence; merged ONCE at config-build per entry; rides the EXISTING ADR-0239 `cluster.WithSubsetMatch` ctx seam UNCHANGED — NO `loadBalancer.Pick` change, NO exported-cluster change — the 38.1 compose). New types: `weightedSelector` + `H1/H2WeightedClusterAction` in `internal/filter/http/router/router_weighted.go` + `weightedClusterRouteAction` bridge in `internal/filter/hcm/actions.go` + `buildWeightedRouterAction`/`mergeRouteSubsetMatch`/`buildWeightedBridge` in `internal/filter/hcm/config.go`. SIX producer parse-reject arms (empty clusters / name-required / cluster_header-unsupported / weight-required / sum-0 / dangling — all unit-level; NO boot-reject fixture). Differential `0065-weighted-clusters` (per-side weight-distribution band over 3 clusters at ~4.5σ margins + a metadata_match-composition arm + cross-side conservation; 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks [swap-weights / drop-cluster / drop-compose]; 20/20 flake-free). ZERO new stat surface (surface 1125 → 1125 — the FIRST LB-family leg to add NO stat surface). The ADR-0241 §Decision/§Consequences bodies landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044. Charters the project's **FIFTH LB construct** and the family's **FIRST wrapper**: `Cluster.lb_subset_config` (`envoy.config.cluster.v3`) — **NOT a `Cluster.LbPolicy` enum value** (unlike the five incumbents roundRobin/leastRequest/randomLB/ringHashLB/maglevLB) but a wrapper triggered by the presence of `lb_subset_config`, that selects a subset of endpoints matching the route's `metadata_match` and **delegates to the cluster's `lb_policy` as the child policy run within that subset**. Picked from the 4-candidate roster (Q0) as **the only candidate decoupled from the absent health-checking boundary** (the other three — locality-weighted / priority / panic — are health-gated and belong after the Upstream-robustness family). As chartered: a `subsetLB` wrapper (`internal/cluster/subset.go` — enumerate subsets per selector × distinct endpoint-metadata value-tuples; a child `loadBalancer` per subset built by the existing policy factory — **ALL FIVE child policies supported** [Q1=A], the hashKey passing through to a hash-policy child; resolve the route match → subset → delegate `child.Pick`; the cluster-level fallback {NO_FALLBACK/ANY_ENDPOINT/DEFAULT_SUBSET} + `default_subset`) over a NEW `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension (the `envoy.lb` namespace captured at `extractEndpoints`, `manager.go:533` — the FIRST per-endpoint dimension beyond `Host:Port`) + a SECOND LB pick-seam input (the resolved subset match widening `Pick(hashKey, hasHash)` → `Pick(hashKey, hasHash, match, hasMatch)` + `WithSubsetMatch`/`subsetMatchFrom`, the ADR-0235 hash-key STYLE TEMPLATE) + a NEW HTTP producer plane (`RouteAction.metadata_match["envoy.lb"]` attached to `clusterRouteAction` — `actions.go:201`). The manager wraps-after-switch (subset wraps the `lb_policy` child — NOT a new `case`). **NO lb_policy reject-text change** (subset is not an enum value — the phase-34/35/36/37 doubly-hit reject-text-retarget lineage BREAKS; the reject surface shifts to `lb_subset_config` validation, D-SS1). **Pre-authorized 38.1/38.2 by-plane split (Q2/Q2b — the phase-36 valve):** 38.1 = subset LB on the HTTP `RouteAction.metadata_match` plane (this leg); 38.2 = `RouteAction.weighted_clusters` routing + `WeightedCluster.ClusterWeight.metadata_match` (a NEW per-request weighted-cluster-selection primitive — the router resolves a SINGLE cluster per route today; the brainstorm surfaced this hidden prerequisite). Differential `0064-lb-subset` (HTTP routes each carrying a `metadata_match` pinned to a subset over `envoy.lb`-tagged endpoints): subset affinity (every served backend ∈ the expected subset, BOTH sides — the SET-membership property; the `metadata_match` is STATIC config → NAT-transparent → TRUE cross-side) + a fallback arm + cross-side byte-equiv + a cross-side `StatsAsserter` (the `lb_subsets_*` stats). 2 ADRs anticipated: **ADR-0239** (the subset-match seam-input extension + the `RouteAction.metadata_match` producer — the seam-BUILD half, parallel to ADR-0235) + **ADR-0240** (the subset LB policy + the `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension — the policy half, parallel to ADR-0236/0238); the phase-36 two-ADR shape (contrast phases 35/37's single-ADR reuse); §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. Anticipated moves at the 38.1 IMPL: stat surface **1121 → ~1125** (~+4 `lb_subsets_*` stats — D-SS4 pins), fixtures **65 → 66** (`0064`; NO boot-reject dir anticipated — the parse rejects land unit-level), fuzzers UNCHANGED **42** (no wire decode — a subset-resolution property test is unit-level), BackendKind tail UNCHANGED **33** (`0064` reuses the `0063` HTTP backends), ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod deps. **The Load-balancing family stays OPEN** — **3** candidates remain after 38 {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds} (all health-gated). REPRODUCED BY EXECUTION at `638ef78a` (positive control `GET /` -> `routed-ok` 200, so the miss is not vacuous): `GET //foo` -> **404** (empty path — routing miss); `GET //foo/bar` -> **SILENT MIS-ROUTE to `/bar`** (leading segment swallowed as authority; served against the WRONG path with no error); mid-path `/a//b` -> 200 (unaffected). ⚠️ **The defect is H2-SPECIFIC** — the IDENTICAL `//foo` over HTTP/1.1 returns `routed-ok` 200 (H1 is served by Go `net/http`; H3 delegates request-target parsing to the `quic-go`/`http3` library), so the blast radius is exactly ONE production site (the `url.Parse(path)` call in `buildRequest`). The fix primitive is `url.ParseRequestURI` (measured at this tip: preserves `//foo`, keeps asterisk-form `*`, splits the query) — to be settled at the SPEC by prototype. The two reproduced arms (404 + the mis-route) are the row's RED anchors; the mis-route's proof must assert the ROUTED PATH, not just the status.
